No limit for paying users.\\n\\n## 2024-10-18\\n\\n_Fixes_\\n\\n- Fixed issue that caused some runs to stall for a long time without getting results. This happened when the input of Instagram Scraper defined `\\\"search\\\": \\\"any_keyword\\\", \\\"searchType\\\": \\\"hashtag\\\"`. Since users only pay for results, there wasn't any extra cost for the user.\\n- Fixed a very rare issue where some runs could stall and continue printing `RequestQueue2: The request queue seems to be stuck`. We reverted to the version of queue that doesn't have this issue.\\n\\n## 2024-10-15\\n\\n_Fixes_\\n\\n- Instagram Hashtag Scraper was fully restored to the same functionality as before 9th of October. You can again receive 100s of thousands of results per hashtag and the order is mostly sorted from newest to oldest.\\n\\n## 2024-10-11\\n\\n_Fixes_\\n\\n- Improved number of results for Instagram Hashtag Scraper, especially when `Number of posts per hashtag` (`resultsLimit`) was small.\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- Added `Only scrape posts with exact hashtag in` (`onlyPostsWithHashtagIn`) to Instagram Hashtag Scraper input. You can choose `hashtags` to require the exact hashtag be present or `hashtag-or-text` where the keyword can be in the caption of the post. `hashtags` is the default value and the previous behavior.\\n\\n## 2024-10-09\\n\\n_Fixes_\\n\\n- **Important**: Instagram put hashtag scraping fully behind login. To work around this issue, Instagram Hashtag Scraper now uses Google Search to find posts with specific hashtags. This means you will get different amount and order of results. The team is examining options to restore the original functionality.\\n\\n## 2024-10-03\\n\\n_Fixes_\\n\\n- Only comments available without login are `latestComments` from post details, so amount even more limited than before and comments scraper just forwarding latest comments as separate dataset items\\n\\n## 2024-08-05\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- Don't retry requests on removed profiles, improve speed\\n\\n## 2024-06-17\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- Restricted profiles resolved with partial details\\n\\n## 2024-04-23\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- Profile search results from ThreadsNet autocomplete appended to Facebook Ads results and continued by Google Search\\n- Added value `searchSource` (`instagram`, `threads`, `facebook-ads` or `google`) to dataset items saved from search\\n\\n## 2024-04-02\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- New API format to parse reels like https://www.instagram.com/reel/C4rQqstCWTQ/\\n\\n## 2024-02-07\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- Added `coauthorProducers` to posts and reels, sample https://www.instagram.com/p/C2vSQcOPkf6/ - note that web page show only first coauthor i.e. \\\"lekkercoffee*rino\\\" but in fact it might be more, i.e. https://www.instagram.com/cocoffeeshops/ feed includes sample \\\"C2vSQcOPkf6\\\"\\n  \\\\_Fixes*\\n- Post-reel hashtags and mentions parsed from parent comment(s) by owner, i.e. https://www.instagram.com/p/C2vSQcOPkf6/ and https://www.instagram.com/p/C2P_5CLRgWS/\\n\\n## 2023-11-03\\n\\n_Features_\\n\\n- Added `enhanceUserSearchWithFacebookPage` that enables enhancing user search with Facebook data that sometimes contains business emails. Scraper\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/td\\u003e\\n\\u003c/tr\\u003e\\n\\u003ctr\\u003e\\n\\u003ctd\\u003e👥 \\u003ca href=\\\"https://apify.com/apify/instagram-followers-count-scraper\\\"\\u003eInstagram Followers Count Scraper\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/td\\u003e\\n\\u003ctd\\u003e🔎 \\u003ca href=\\\"https://apify.com/apify/instagram-search-scraper\\\"\\u003eInstagram Search Scraper\\u003c/a\\u003e\\u003c/td\\u003e\\n\\u003c/tr\\u003e\\n\\u003c/table\\u003e\\n\\n\\u003e If you're comfortable with more complex settings, use our more advanced 🔗 [Instagram Scraper](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-scraper) or 🔗 [Instagram API Scraper](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-api-scraper). They cover almost all functionalities of the dedicated scrapers.\\n\\n## ❓FAQ\\n\\n### How to scrape trial reels?\\n\\nTrial reels are an Instagram feature that creators use to test a video with non-followers before sharing it with their existing audience. Here's how you can include or exclude trial reels from our output:\\n\\n- Keep trial reels (default): the feed is scraped with trial reels mixed in among regular reels.\\n- Skip trial reels: enable the skip option, and the feed is scraped without them, which gives you cleaner, more accurate results.\\n\\nTrial reels aren't marked differently in the output, so you can't tell them apart from regular reels within a single run. To find out which reels are trial, scrape the same profile twice, once with trial reels and once without, then compare the two datasets. Any reel that shows up only in the \\\"keep\\\" run is a trial reel. Tried it out? Share your feedback in a [review](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-reel-scraper/reviews).\\n\\n### Sometimes the scraped metrics (like, comment, and view counts) don’t match with what is shown on Instagram. Why is that?\\n\\nIf you compare a post or reel side by side with the Actor's output, the numbers can differ. There are two common reasons, and neither means the data is wrong.\\n\\n1. The Actor sees the logged-out version of the page. The Actor scrapes public data, so it gets the same counts Instagram shows a visitor who isn't signed in. Instagram doesn't show logged-out visitors everything: engagement from private accounts, for example, is often hidden. That means the counts you see while logged into your own account can be higher than the ones the Actor returns.\\n   To check what the Actor should see, open the post in a private/incognito browser window. The counts there should match the Actor's output. If they do, the Actor is working correctly.\\n\\n2. Counts are a snapshot from the moment of the run. Views, likes, and comments keep changing after the run finishes. A reel that returned 976 views in the morning can legitimately show 982 views when you check it in the afternoon. Always compare against the run's timestamp, not against the live page hours or days later. If the numbers still don't line up after both checks, contact support with the run ID and the post URL so we can look into it.\\n\\n### Why did we introduce `downloadedVideo` URLs to download reels instead of relying on CDN-based video links?\\n\\n`downloadedVideo` URLs are more reliable and globally accessible, with predictable expiration (downloaded video expires after 3 days) and no IP blocks. While CDN URLs were a cheaper option, they also expire unpredictably, are region-restricted, cause IP blocks on large runs, and sometimes separate audio from video which is not what users would want from their downloaded reels.\\n\\n### Can I track reel metrics over time?\\n\\nYes. Metrics like views, likes, comments, and shares are included in each scrape. You can store and track them over time to monitor performance.\\n\\n### Can I scrape multiple reels at once?\\n\\nYes. You can provide a list of reel URLs or an Instagram profile URL to scrape all Reels from that profile.\\n\\n### Sometimes the scraped metrics (like, comment, and view counts) don’t match with what is shown on Instagram. Why is that?\\n\\nIf you compare a post or reel side by side with the Actor's output, the numbers can differ. There are two common reasons, and neither means the data is wrong.\\n\\n1. The Actor sees the logged-out version of the page. The Actor scrapes public data, so it gets the same counts Instagram shows a visitor who isn't signed in. Instagram doesn't show logged-out visitors everything: engagement from private accounts, for example, is often hidden. That means the counts you see while logged into your own account can be higher than the ones the Actor returns.\\n   To check what the Actor should see, open the post in a private/incognito browser window. The counts there should match the Actor's output. If they do, the Actor is working correctly.\\n\\n2. Counts are a snapshot from the moment of the run. Views, likes, and comments keep changing after the run finishes. A reel that returned 976 views in the morning can legitimately show 982 views when you check it in the afternoon. Always compare against the run's timestamp, not against the live page hours or days later. If the numbers still don't line up after both checks, contact support with the run ID and the post URL so we can look into it.\\n\\n### Are comments included in the reels scraping?\\n\\nYes, the latest 10 comments are included, along with replies, likes, and timestamps. You can also see if comments are disabled for a reel. If you want to scrape all comments from a reel, use 💬 [Instagram Comments Scraper](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-comment-scraper).\\n\\n### Can I scrape reels from private accounts?\\n\\nNo. The scraper only works for public reels. Private reels content will not be accessible.\\n\\n### Can I skip pinned reels when scraping Instagram reels?\\n\\nYes. You can choose whether to include or exclude pinned reels when scraping. Since pinned reels can sometimes be very old (and not relevant to your scrape), skipping them just makes sense sometimes. However, this option is only available in the JSON input, not in the user-facing UI. You can control it by setting it to `true`or `false`. For more details, check the [Input tab](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-post-scraper/input-schema) of the Actor.\\n\\n### Can I exclude pinned reels even if they are within the date range?\\n\\nThat’s not possible to filter out before scraping. The scraper will always include pinned reels if they match your Date filter settings. If you want to exclude them, you’ll need to filter the output data after the run by checking for `isPinned: true` and removing those items.\\n\\n### Is there any way to scrape only new reels (including pinned ones) while filtering out older pinned reels?\\n\\nYes, there is. You can combine the **Date filter** with **Pinned posts** parameter in JSON input. If you want to see reels newer than a certain date (using the Date filter `onlyPostsNewerThan`) and also include Pinned reels only if they meet that condition, set:\\n\\n```json\\n\\\"skipPinnedPosts\\\": true,\\n\\\"onlyPostsNewerThan\\\": \\\"1 day\\\"\\n```\\n\\nWith this setup:\\n\\n- Pinned reels that are newer than the time restriction will still be included.\\n- Older pinned reels will be skipped.\\n\\nThis way you’ll get the result you want: “Scrape pinned reels only if they fit the date restriction”.\\n\\n### How is video data returned?\\n\\nVideos are returned as direct MP4 URLs. You also get all associated images for the reel in multiple resolutions.\\n\\n### Is there support for original audio?\\n\\nYes. You get full details on the audio, including artist, song name, and whether the original audio is used.\\n\\n### Is it legal to scrape Instagram reels?\\n\\nOur Instagram Reel scraper only collects data that is publicly available on Instagram. It does not access private content. You should be aware that some public data may be considered personal data under regulations like the [GDPR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/General_Data_Protection_Regulation). Only scrape data if you have a legitimate reason. For guidance, see our blog on [the legality of web scraping](https://blog.apify.com/is-web-scraping-legal/).\\n\\n### Can I integrate Instagram Reel data with other services?\\n\\nYes. Scraped reel data can be integrated with almost any cloud service or web app. Apify supports **Zapier, n8n, Slack, Make, Airbyte, Gumloop, CrewAI, IFTTT, Lindy, GitHub, Google Sheets, Google Drive**, [and more](https://apify.com/integrations). You can also use [webhooks](https://docs.apify.com/integrations/webhooks) to trigger actions whenever a run finishes, like sending notifications or syncing datasets.\\n\\n### Can I use the Instagram Reel Scraper with the Apify API?\\n\\nYes. The Apify API provides programmatic access to run Actors, fetch datasets, monitor performance, and manage tasks. For Node.js, use the apify-client [NPM package](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-reel-scraper/api/javascript). For Python, use the apify-client [PyPI package](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-reel-scraper/api/python). Full API docs are available in the [Apify API reference](https://docs.apify.com/api/v2).\\n\\n### Can I get Instagram Reel details through an MCP server?\\n\\nYes. You can [connect](https://apify.com/apify/instagram-reel-scraper/api/mcp) to an MCP server using clients like ClaudeDesktop or LibreChat, or build your own integration. For Instagram Reel Scraper:\\n\\n1. Start a Server-Sent Events (SSE) session to receive a `sessionId`.\\n2. Send API messages using that `sessionId` to trigger the scraper.\\n3. The message starts the Instagram Reel Scraper with the provided input.\\n4. The response should be: Accepted.\\n\\nLearn more in [MCP setup guide](https://blog.apify.com/how-to-use-mcp/).\\n\\n### Instagram Reel Scraper not working\\n\\nThe Apify team constantly improves Actor performance.
